{"forms":[{"form":"plucks","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"plucking","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"plucked","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"plucked","tags":["past"]},{"form":"pluckt","tags":["obsolete","participle","past"]},{"form":"pluckt","tags":["obsolete","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"She plucked the phone from her bag and dialled.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To pull something sharply; to pull something out"],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To take or remove (someone) quickly from a particular place or situation."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Whereas a piano strikes the string, a harpsichord plucks it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To play (a single string on a musical instrument) by pulling and then releasing it, such as on a guitar."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"glosses":["To remove feathers from (a bird)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"defeather"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To rob, steal from; to cheat or swindle (someone)."],"tags":["archaic","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Plucking a bow instrument may cause a string to break.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To play a string instrument pizzicato."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to pluck at somebody's sleeve","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To pull or twitch sharply."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To reject (a student) after they fail an examination for a degree."],"qualifier":"university slang","tags":["UK","transitive"]},{"glosses":["Of a glacier: to transport individual pieces of bedrock by means of gradual erosion through freezing and thawing."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/plʌk/"},{"ipa":"/plʊk/","tags":["Ireland","Northern-England"]}],"upstream_line":254675,"upstream_offset":1265674389,"upstream_record_sha256":"38e32858288ca71c785d472ff0fab3a47c39aee74d5cbb574757401feeb17c82","word":"pluck"}
